- **anti-slop-plugin**: PostToolUse on Write|Edit. Anti-slop house style gate. After any Write or Edit, runs the voice linter at ${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/../anti-slop-brain/scripts/lint_voice.py over the file that was just written. On a house style violation it exits 2, which blocks and feeds the linter output back for self-correction. - **arbiter**: PreToolUse, SessionStart on Read, startup. 两个 hook。① PreToolUse(Read)：Read 一个 .docx / .xlsx / .doc / .odt / .epub 时，先用 arbiter CLI 把它转成一份临时 Markdown/CSV/文本，再把这次读取改写到那份临时产物上——这是**改写**不是拦截，找不到应用、没有 CLI、引擎没装、转换失败、超时，任何一条不成立都原样放行。② SessionStart(startup)：看一眼 Arbiter 有没有新版本，有就提一句；没网、没新版、任何异常都**完全静默**（空 stdout + 退出码 0），绝不拖慢会话启动。 ([Polaris-bit189/Arbiter](https://github.com/Polaris-bit189/Arbiter/blob/HEAD/plugins/arbiter/hooks/hooks.json) · ★4)
